"""走势类型 Gate B：严格中枢关系与结构候选（不判断完成）。"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, replace
from typing import Any

from manual_rebuild.algo.bi import bi_confirmed_flags
from manual_rebuild.algo.config import ChanConfig
from manual_rebuild.algo.models import (
    Bi,
    BiDir,
    CompletedZouShi,
    LevelBuildResult,
    LevelLeg,
    Xianduan,
    ZhongShu,
    ZhongShuMode,
    ZhongShuRelation,
    ZhongShuRelationType,
    ZhongShuSource,
    ZhongShuState,
    ZouShiBoundaryState,
    ZouShiCandidate,
    ZouShiClassificationState,
    ZouShiDirection,
    ZouShiEndpoint,
    ZouShiKind,
    ZouShiLevel,
    ZouShiStability,
)
from manual_rebuild.algo.same_level import build_same_level_result
from manual_rebuild.algo.trace import (
    RULE_ZOUSHI_ONE_CENTER,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_DOWN,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_DOWN_EXPANSION,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_UNDETERMINED,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_UP,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_UP_EXPANSION,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_TREND_QUALIFIED,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FORMING,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_CONSOLIDATION,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_EXIT_BREAK,
    RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_TREND,
    StructTrace,
)
from manual_rebuild.algo.zhongshu import select_l1_frozen_xianduan_prefix
from manual_rebuild.algo.zoushi_kernel import (
    ZouShiKernelCenter,
    ZouShiKernelLeg,
    ZouShiKernelRuleIds,
    ZouShiKernelSlice,
    build_zoushi_kernel_slices,
)

ZOUSHI_ALGO_VERSION = 2
ZOUSHI_V2_ALGO_VERSION = 21
ZOUSHI_DISABLED_REASON = "zoushi_requires_standard_l1_frozen_xianduan_zhongshu"
_ALGO_CARD_ID = "zoushi-v1"
_V2_ALGO_CARD_ID = "zoushi-v2"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ZouShiBuildResult:
    """同一次纯函数派生的相邻关系与结构候选。"""

    relations: tuple[ZhongShuRelation]
    candidates: tuple[ZouShiCandidate]


def classify_zhongshu_relation(
    left: ZhongShu, right: ZhongShu
) -> ZhongShuRelationType:
    """按第 20 课中心定理二分类；严格不等号，等号不算趋势。"""
    if right.dd > left.gg:
        return ZhongShuRelationType.UP_TREND
    if right.gg < left.dd:
        return ZhongShuRelationType.DOWN_TREND
    if right.zd > left.zg and right.dd <= left.gg:
        return ZhongShuRelationType.UP_EXPANSION
    if right.zg < left.zd and right.gg >= left.dd:
        return ZhongShuRelationType.DOWN_EXPANSION
    return ZhongShuRelationType.UNDETERMINED


def _stability(items: Sequence[ZhongShu]) -> ZouShiStability:
    return (
        ZouShiStability.STABLE
        if all(item.extension_state is ZhongShuState.CLOSED for item in items)
        else ZouShiStability.PROVISIONAL
    )


def _trace(
    items: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    *,
    rule_id: str,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> StructTrace:
    traces = [item.trace for item in items if item.trace is not None]
    source_from = [
        trace.source_from_ts
        for trace in traces
        if trace.source_from_ts is not None
    ]
    source_to = [
        trace.source_to_ts for trace in traces if trace.source_to_ts is not None
    ]
    merged_from = [
        trace.merged_from_index
        for trace in traces
        if trace.merged_from_index is not None
    ]
    merged_to = [
        trace.merged_to_index
        for trace in traces
        if trace.merged_to_index is not None
    ]
    dep_to = [
        trace.dep_to_index for trace in traces if trace.dep_to_index is not None
    ]
    return StructTrace(
        algo_card_id=_ALGO_CARD_ID,
        rule_id=rule_id,
        profile_id=profile_id,
        config_hash=config_hash,
        source_from_ts=min(source_from) if source_from else min(item.start_ts for item in items),
        source_to_ts=max(source_to) if source_to else max(item.end_ts for item in items),
        merged_from_index=min(merged_from) if merged_from else None,
        merged_to_index=max(merged_to) if merged_to else None,
        dep_to_index=max(dep_to) if dep_to else None,
    )


_RELATION_RULES = {
    ZhongShuRelationType.UP_TREND: RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_UP,
    ZhongShuRelationType.DOWN_TREND: RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_DOWN,
    ZhongShuRelationType.UP_EXPANSION: RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_UP_EXPANSION,
    ZhongShuRelationType.DOWN_EXPANSION: RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_DOWN_EXPANSION,
    ZhongShuRelationType.UNDETERMINED: RULE_ZOUSHI_RELATION_UNDETERMINED,
}


def _relation(
    left: ZhongShu,
    right: ZhongShu,
    left_index: int,
    *,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> ZhongShuRelation:
    relation = classify_zhongshu_relation(left, right)
    return ZhongShuRelation(
        period=left.period,
        source_type=ZhongShuSource.XIANDUAN,
        relation=relation,
        stability=_stability((left, right)),
        left_zhongshu_id=left.id,
        right_zhongshu_id=right.id,
        left_index=left_index,
        right_index=left_index + 1,
        start_ts=left.start_ts,
        end_ts=right.end_ts,
        left_zg=left.zg,
        left_zd=left.zd,
        left_gg=left.gg,
        left_dd=left.dd,
        right_zg=right.zg,
        right_zd=right.zd,
        right_gg=right.gg,
        right_dd=right.dd,
        trace=_trace(
            (left, right),
            rule_id=_RELATION_RULES[relation],
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        ),
    )


def _direction(relation: ZhongShuRelationType) -> ZouShiDirection | None:
    if relation is ZhongShuRelationType.UP_TREND:
        return ZouShiDirection.UP
    if relation is ZhongShuRelationType.DOWN_TREND:
        return ZouShiDirection.DOWN
    return None


def _candidate(
    centers: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    relations: Sequence[ZhongShuRelation],
    start: int,
    end: int,
    direction: ZouShiDirection | None,
    *,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> ZouShiCandidate:
    members = centers[start : end + 1]
    member_relations = relations[start:end]
    kind = ZouShiKind.TREND if len(members) >= 2 else ZouShiKind.CONSOLIDATION
    rule_id = RULE_ZOUSHI_TREND_QUALIFIED if kind is ZouShiKind.TREND else RULE_ZOUSHI_ONE_CENTER
    return ZouShiCandidate(
        period=members[0].period,
        kind=kind,
        trend_direction=direction,
        classification_state=ZouShiClassificationState.STRUCTURALLY_QUALIFIED,
        stability=_stability(members),
        start_ts=members[0].start_ts,
        end_ts=members[-1].end_ts,
        start_zhongshu_index=start,
        end_zhongshu_index=end,
        zhongshu_ids=tuple(item.id for item in members),
        relation_ids=tuple(item.id for item in member_relations),
        member_count=len(members),
        trace=_trace(
            members,
            rule_id=rule_id,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        ),
    )


def build_zoushi_candidates(
    zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    *,
    period: str,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> ZouShiBuildResult:
    """从正式段中枢构建关系与最大同向局部候选；不判断走势完成。"""
    centers = list(zhongshus)
    for index, item in enumerate(centers):
        if item.source_type is not ZhongShuSource.XIANDUAN:
            raise ValueError("formal zoushi candidates require xianduan zhongshu input")
        if item.period != period:
            raise ValueError(f"zhongshu period {item.period!r} != requested {period!r}")
        if index and item.start_ts <= centers[index - 1].start_ts:
            raise ValueError("zhongshu inputs must be strictly ordered by start_ts")
    if not centers:
        return ZouShiBuildResult(relations=(), candidates=())

    relations = tuple(
        _relation(
            centers[index],
            centers[index + 1],
            index,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        )
        for index in range(len(centers) - 1)
    )
    candidates: list[ZouShiCandidate] = []
    chain_start = 0
    chain_direction: ZouShiDirection | None = None
    for index, relation in enumerate(relations):
        next_direction = _direction(relation.relation)
        if next_direction is None:
            candidates.append(
                _candidate(
                    centers,
                    relations,
                    chain_start,
                    index,
                    chain_direction,
                    profile_id=profile_id,
                    config_hash=config_hash,
                )
            )
            chain_start = index + 1
            chain_direction = None
        elif chain_direction is None:
            chain_direction = next_direction
        elif chain_direction is not next_direction:
            candidates.append(
                _candidate(
                    centers,
                    relations,
                    chain_start,
                    index,
                    chain_direction,
                    profile_id=profile_id,
                    config_hash=config_hash,
                )
            )
            # Gate B 是局部结构候选而非完成走势分解；反转关系共享转折中枢。
            chain_start = index
            chain_direction = next_direction
    candidates.append(
        _candidate(
            centers,
            relations,
            chain_start,
            len(centers) - 1,
            chain_direction,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        )
    )
    return ZouShiBuildResult(relations=relations, candidates=tuple(candidates))

# ── v2：L0/L1 共用的同级别分解内核 ──────────────────────────────────────


def _level_endpoint(leg: LevelLeg, *, at_start: bool) -> ZouShiEndpoint:
    return ZouShiEndpoint(
        source_id=leg.source_id,
        source_index=leg.source_index,
        ts=leg.start_ts if at_start else leg.end_ts,
        price=leg.start_price if at_start else leg.end_price,
    )


def _level_continuous(left: LevelLeg, right: LevelLeg) -> bool:
    return (
        right.source_index == left.source_index + 1
        and left.end_ts == right.start_ts
        and left.direction is not right.direction
    )


def _leg_position(
    source_position: dict[int, int],
    source_index: int | None,
    *,
    role: str,
) -> int:
    """把正式中枢保存的来源下标解析到当前连续部件序列。"""
    if source_index is None:
        raise ValueError(f"official zhongshu is missing {role}")
    try:
        return source_position[source_index]
    except KeyError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"official zhongshu {role} is outside the supplied leg run") from exc


def _direction_extreme_leg(
    run: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    *,
    start_position: int,
    end_position: int,
    direction: ZouShiDirection,
) -> LevelLeg:
    """取中枢生命周期内同方向离开尝试的极值终点；同价取最后一次。"""
    bi_direction = BiDir.UP if direction is ZouShiDirection.UP else BiDir.DOWN
    attempts = [
        leg
        for leg in run[start_position : end_position + 1]
        if leg.direction is bi_direction
    ]
    if not attempts:
        raise ValueError("zoushi lifecycle has no directional exit-attempt leg")
    if direction is ZouShiDirection.UP:
        return max(attempts, key=lambda leg: (leg.end_price, leg.source_index))
    return min(attempts, key=lambda leg: (leg.end_price, -leg.source_index))


def _trace_from_dependencies(
    dependencies: Sequence[Any],
    *,
    rule_id: str,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> StructTrace:
    traces = [item.trace for item in dependencies if item.trace is not None]
    source_from = [
        trace.source_from_ts
        for trace in traces
        if trace.source_from_ts is not None
    ]
    source_to = [
        trace.source_to_ts for trace in traces if trace.source_to_ts is not None
    ]
    merged_from = [
        trace.merged_from_index
        for trace in traces
        if trace.merged_from_index is not None
    ]
    merged_to = [
        trace.merged_to_index
        for trace in traces
        if trace.merged_to_index is not None
    ]
    dep_to = [
        trace.dep_to_index for trace in traces if trace.dep_to_index is not None
    ]
    return StructTrace(
        algo_card_id=_V2_ALGO_CARD_ID,
        rule_id=rule_id,
        profile_id=profile_id,
        config_hash=config_hash,
        source_from_ts=(
            min(source_from)
            if source_from
            else min(item.start_ts for item in dependencies)
        ),
        source_to_ts=(
            max(source_to)
            if source_to
            else max(item.end_ts for item in dependencies)
        ),
        merged_from_index=min(merged_from) if merged_from else None,
        merged_to_index=max(merged_to) if merged_to else None,
        dep_to_index=max(dep_to) if dep_to else None,
    )


def _zhongshu_strict_direction(
    left: ZhongShu, right: ZhongShu
) -> ZouShiDirection | None:
    if right.dd > left.gg:
        return ZouShiDirection.UP
    if right.gg < left.dd:
        return ZouShiDirection.DOWN
    return None


def _make_level_zoushi(
    run: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    member_zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    *,
    direction: ZouShiDirection | None,
    evidence: ZhongShu | None,
    source_evidence: LevelLeg | None = None,
    exit_direction_override: ZouShiDirection | None,
    start_position_override: int | None,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> CompletedZouShi:
    if evidence is not None and source_evidence is not None:
        raise ValueError("zoushi close evidence must be zhongshu or source leg, not both")
    source_position = {leg.source_index: index for index, leg in enumerate(run)}
    first_center = member_zhongshus[0]
    last_center = member_zhongshus[-1]
    natural_start_position = _leg_position(
        source_position,
        first_center.entry_evidence_index,
        role="entry_evidence_index",
    )
    start_position = (
        natural_start_position
        if start_position_override is None
        else start_position_override
    )
    if not 0 <= start_position < len(run):
        raise ValueError("shared zoushi start is outside the supplied leg run")
    start_leg = run[start_position]
    if evidence is None and source_evidence is None:
        boundary_position = None
        completion_state = ZouShiBoundaryState.FORMING
        close_evidence_id = None
        close_evidence_source_id = None
        observed_members = list(run[start_position:])
        dependencies: list[Any] = [*observed_members, *member_zhongshus]
        rule_id = RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FORMING
    elif evidence is not None:
        boundary_position = _leg_position(
            source_position,
            evidence.start_index,
            role="close_evidence_start_index",
        )
        boundary_end_position = _leg_position(
            source_position,
            evidence.end_index,
            role="close_evidence_end_index",
        )
        completion_state = ZouShiBoundaryState.FROZEN
        close_evidence_id = evidence.id
        close_evidence_source_id = None
        dependencies = [
            *run[start_position : boundary_end_position + 1],
            *member_zhongshus,
        ]
        rule_id = (
            RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_TREND
            if direction is not None
            else RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_CONSOLIDATION
        )
    else:
        assert source_evidence is not None
        boundary_position = source_position[source_evidence.source_index]
        boundary_end_position = boundary_position
        completion_state = ZouShiBoundaryState.FROZEN
        close_evidence_id = None
        close_evidence_source_id = source_evidence.source_id
        dependencies = [
            *run[start_position : boundary_end_position + 1],
            *member_zhongshus,
        ]
        rule_id = RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_EXIT_BREAK
    last_center_start = max(
        start_position,
        _leg_position(
            source_position,
            last_center.entry_evidence_index,
            role="last_member_entry_evidence_index",
        ),
    )
    # 右侧中枢只是确认“旧走势不再延续”的证据，不属于旧走势成员。
    # 旧走势的极值仍可出现在最后成员中枢结束之后、右证据核心开始之时，
    # 因此 frozen 搜索到 evidence.start_index（含）即可；完整 evidence 只进 trace。
    if evidence is None and source_evidence is None:
        lifecycle_end = len(run) - 1
    else:
        assert boundary_position is not None
        lifecycle_end = boundary_position
    if exit_direction_override is not None:
        exit_direction = exit_direction_override
    elif direction is not None:
        exit_direction = direction
    elif evidence is None:
        exit_direction = (
            ZouShiDirection.UP
            if run[-1].direction is BiDir.UP
            else ZouShiDirection.DOWN
        )
    else:
        raise ValueError("frozen consolidation requires an explicit exit direction")
    end_leg = _direction_extreme_leg(
        run,
        start_position=last_center_start,
        end_position=lifecycle_end,
        direction=exit_direction,
    )
    end = _level_endpoint(end_leg, at_start=False)
    end_position = source_position[end_leg.source_index]
    # frozen 的共享中枢极值之后属于下一走势；forming 尚无右侧分界，
    # 因而保留当前已观察到的全部部件作为可变成员。
    source_members = (
        observed_members
        if boundary_position is None
        else list(run[start_position : end_position + 1])
    )
    kind = ZouShiKind.TREND if direction is not None else ZouShiKind.CONSOLIDATION
    return CompletedZouShi(
        period=start_leg.period,
        level=start_leg.level,
        source_type=start_leg.source_type,
        kind=kind,
        trend_direction=direction,
        start=_level_endpoint(start_leg, at_start=True),
        end=end,
        start_source_id=start_leg.source_id,
        source_member_ids=tuple(item.source_id for item in source_members),
        zhongshu_ids=tuple(item.id for item in member_zhongshus),
        completion_state=completion_state,
        close_evidence_zhongshu_id=close_evidence_id,
        close_evidence_source_id=close_evidence_source_id,
        trace=_trace_from_dependencies(
            dependencies,
            rule_id=rule_id,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        ),
    )


def _make_same_level_consolidation(
    run: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    center: ZhongShu,
    *,
    evidence: ZhongShu | None,
    start_position: int,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> CompletedZouShi:
    """构造同级分解中的单中枢盘整。

    第 38 课口径下，相邻重叠中枢不是延伸，而是“盘整 + 盘整”。右中枢
    三核心成立时，左盘整固定结束在自身核心 C 终点；最右活动盘整则继续
    画到当前已确认部件末端，等待下一中枢决定最终边界。
    """
    source_position = {leg.source_index: index for index, leg in enumerate(run)}
    core_end_position = _leg_position(
        source_position,
        center.end_index,
        role="same_level_center_end_index",
    )
    if not 0 <= start_position <= core_end_position:
        raise ValueError("same-level consolidation start is outside its center")
    start_leg = run[start_position]
    if evidence is None:
        end_position = len(run) - 1
        completion_state = ZouShiBoundaryState.FORMING
        close_evidence_id = None
        dependencies: list[Any] = [*run[start_position:]]
        rule_id = RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FORMING
    else:
        end_position = core_end_position
        completion_state = ZouShiBoundaryState.FROZEN
        close_evidence_id = evidence.id
        evidence_end_position = _leg_position(
            source_position,
            evidence.end_index,
            role="same_level_close_evidence_event_index",
        )
        # 依赖水位锁在右中枢核心 C 的确认笔；不要嵌套 evidence.trace，
        # 因为右中枢未来由 open→closed 时其自身 trace 仍会增长。
        dependencies = [*run[start_position : evidence_end_position + 1]]
        rule_id = RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_CONSOLIDATION
    end_leg = run[end_position]
    source_members = run[start_position : end_position + 1]
    return CompletedZouShi(
        period=start_leg.period,
        level=start_leg.level,
        source_type=start_leg.source_type,
        kind=ZouShiKind.CONSOLIDATION,
        trend_direction=None,
        start=_level_endpoint(start_leg, at_start=True),
        end=_level_endpoint(end_leg, at_start=False),
        start_source_id=start_leg.source_id,
        source_member_ids=tuple(item.source_id for item in source_members),
        zhongshu_ids=(center.id,),
        completion_state=completion_state,
        close_evidence_zhongshu_id=close_evidence_id,
        trace=_trace_from_dependencies(
            dependencies,
            rule_id=rule_id,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        ),
    )


def _build_same_level_zoushis_for_run(
    run: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    *,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> list[CompletedZouShi]:
    """第 38 课同级分解：重叠中枢切成盘整+盘整，严格同向才升级趋势。"""
    if not zhongshus:
        return []
    out: list[CompletedZouShi] = []
    source_position = {leg.source_index: index for index, leg in enumerate(run)}
    member_zhongshus: list[ZhongShu] = [zhongshus[0]]
    direction: ZouShiDirection | None = None
    start_position = _leg_position(
        source_position,
        zhongshus[0].entry_evidence_index,
        role="same_level_first_entry_index",
    )

    for item in zhongshus[1:]:
        strict_direction = _zhongshu_strict_direction(member_zhongshus[-1], item)
        if direction is None and strict_direction is not None:
            member_zhongshus.append(item)
            direction = strict_direction
            continue
        if direction is not None and strict_direction is direction:
            member_zhongshus.append(item)
            continue

        if direction is None:
            completed = _make_same_level_consolidation(
                run,
                member_zhongshus[0],
                evidence=item,
                start_position=start_position,
                profile_id=profile_id,
                config_hash=config_hash,
            )
        else:
            completed = _make_level_zoushi(
                run,
                member_zhongshus,
                direction=direction,
                evidence=item,
                exit_direction_override=direction,
                start_position_override=start_position,
                profile_id=profile_id,
                config_hash=config_hash,
            )
            evidence_end_position = _leg_position(
                source_position,
                item.end_index,
                role="same_level_trend_close_event_index",
            )
            completed = replace(
                completed,
                trace=_trace_from_dependencies(
                    run[start_position : evidence_end_position + 1],
                    rule_id=RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_TREND,
                    profile_id=profile_id,
                    config_hash=config_hash,
                ),
            )
        out.append(completed)
        start_position = source_position[completed.end.source_index] + 1
        if start_position >= len(run):
            return out
        member_zhongshus = [item]
        direction = None

    if direction is None:
        out.append(
            _make_same_level_consolidation(
                run,
                member_zhongshus[0],
                evidence=None,
                start_position=start_position,
                profile_id=profile_id,
                config_hash=config_hash,
            )
        )
    else:
        out.append(
            _make_level_zoushi(
                run,
                member_zhongshus,
                direction=direction,
                evidence=None,
                exit_direction_override=direction,
                start_position_override=start_position,
                profile_id=profile_id,
                config_hash=config_hash,
            )
        )
    return out


_LEVEL_KERNEL_RULES = ZouShiKernelRuleIds(
    forming=RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FORMING,
    freeze_trend=RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_TREND,
    freeze_consolidation=RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_CONSOLIDATION,
    freeze_exit_break=RULE_ZOUSHI_V2_FREEZE_EXIT_BREAK,
)


def _kernel_level_leg(item: LevelLeg) -> ZouShiKernelLeg:
    direction = (
        ZouShiDirection.UP if item.direction is BiDir.UP else ZouShiDirection.DOWN
    )
    return ZouShiKernelLeg(
        id=item.source_id,
        position=item.source_index,
        direction=direction,
        start=_level_endpoint(item, at_start=True),
        end=_level_endpoint(item, at_start=False),
        high=item.high,
        low=item.low,
        trace=item.trace,
    )


def _kernel_level_center(
    item: ZhongShu,
    center_position: int,
    source_position: dict[int, int],
) -> ZouShiKernelCenter:
    close_position = (
        None
        if item.close_evidence_index is None
        else _leg_position(
            source_position,
            item.close_evidence_index,
            role="kernel_close_evidence_index",
        )
    )
    return ZouShiKernelCenter(
        id=item.id,
        position=center_position,
        entry_position=_leg_position(
            source_position, item.entry_evidence_index, role="kernel_entry_index"
        ),
        core_start_position=_leg_position(
            source_position, item.start_index, role="kernel_core_start_index"
        ),
        core_event_position=_leg_position(
            source_position, item.start_index + 2, role="kernel_core_event_index"
        ),
        member_end_position=_leg_position(
            source_position, item.end_index, role="kernel_member_end_index"
        ),
        zg=item.zg,
        zd=item.zd,
        gg=item.gg,
        dd=item.dd,
        extension_state=item.extension_state,
        close_evidence_position=close_position,
        member_ids=item.core_member_ids + item.extended_member_ids,
        trace=item.trace,
    )


def _completed_from_kernel_slice(
    run: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    centers: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    center_positions: Sequence[int],
    item: ZouShiKernelSlice,
    *,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> CompletedZouShi:
    leg_by_position = {leg.source_index: leg for leg in run}
    local_by_position = {
        leg.source_index: position for position, leg in enumerate(run)
    }
    center_by_position = dict(zip(center_positions, centers, strict=True))
    start_leg = leg_by_position[item.start_position]
    end_leg = leg_by_position[item.end_position]
    member_centers = [center_by_position[position] for position in item.center_positions]
    evidence_center: ZhongShu | None = None
    evidence_leg: LevelLeg | None = None
    if item.close_evidence is not None:
        if item.close_evidence.kind == "zhongshu":
            evidence_center = next(
                center for center in centers if center.id == item.close_evidence.id
            )
        else:
            evidence_leg = leg_by_position[item.close_evidence.event_position]
    if evidence_center is not None:
        dependency_end = local_by_position[evidence_center.end_index]
    elif evidence_leg is not None:
        dependency_end = local_by_position[item.close_evidence.event_position]  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    else:
        dependency_end = len(run) - 1
    dependency_start = local_by_position[item.start_position]
    dependencies: list[Any] = [
        *run[dependency_start : dependency_end + 1],
        *member_centers,
    ]
    return CompletedZouShi(
        period=start_leg.period,
        level=start_leg.level,
        source_type=start_leg.source_type,
        kind=item.kind,
        trend_direction=item.direction,
        start=_level_endpoint(start_leg, at_start=True),
        end=_level_endpoint(end_leg, at_start=False),
        start_source_id=start_leg.source_id,
        source_member_ids=tuple(
            leg_by_position[position].source_id for position in item.source_positions
        ),
        zhongshu_ids=tuple(center.id for center in member_centers),
        completion_state=item.completion_state,
        close_evidence_zhongshu_id=(
            evidence_center.id if evidence_center is not None else None
        ),
        close_evidence_source_id=(evidence_leg.source_id if evidence_leg is not None else None),
        trace=_trace_from_dependencies(
            dependencies,
            rule_id=item.primary_rule_id,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        ),
    )


def _build_extend_zoushis_with_kernel(
    run: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    center_positions: Sequence[int],
    *,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> list[CompletedZouShi]:
    if len(zhongshus) != len(center_positions):
        raise ValueError("kernel center positions must match official zhongshus")
    source_position = {leg.source_index: leg.source_index for leg in run}
    kernel_legs = tuple(_kernel_level_leg(leg) for leg in run)
    kernel_centers = tuple(
        _kernel_level_center(center, position, source_position)
        for position, center in zip(center_positions, zhongshus, strict=True)
    )
    return [
        _completed_from_kernel_slice(
            run,
            zhongshus,
            center_positions,
            item,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        )
        for item in build_zoushi_kernel_slices(
            kernel_legs,
            kernel_centers,
            rules=_LEVEL_KERNEL_RULES,
        )
    ]


def _build_zoushis_for_run(
    run: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    center_positions: Sequence[int],
    *,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> list[CompletedZouShi]:
    if not zhongshus:
        return []
    modes = {item.mode for item in zhongshus}
    if len(modes) != 1:
        raise ValueError("official zhongshus must share one decomposition mode")
    if ZhongShuMode.SAME_LEVEL in modes:
        return _build_same_level_zoushis_for_run(
            run,
            zhongshus,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        )
    return _build_extend_zoushis_with_kernel(
        run,
        zhongshus,
        center_positions,
        profile_id=profile_id,
        config_hash=config_hash,
    )

def build_zoushis_from_zhongshus(
    legs: Sequence[LevelLeg],
    zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    *,
    level: ZouShiLevel,
    source_type: ZhongShuSource,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
    zhongshu_mode: ZhongShuMode | None = None,
) -> LevelBuildResult:
    """从中枢层正式结果构建走势，不允许静默替换正式中枢。

    ``same_level`` 的 Engine 正式路径复用一次共享构建结果；本兼容纯函数
    入口只重放同一状态机以逐对象校验传入中枢，任何差异都显式拒绝。
    当正式中枢可能为空时，调用方必须显式传入 ``zhongshu_mode``，避免
    空列表丢失其口径身份。
    """
    resolved_level = ZouShiLevel(level)
    resolved_source = ZhongShuSource(source_type)
    source = list(legs)
    centers = list(zhongshus)
    resolved_mode = ZhongShuMode(zhongshu_mode) if zhongshu_mode is not None else None
    if any(
        item.level is not resolved_level or item.source_type is not resolved_source
        for item in source
    ):
        raise ValueError("level legs must share the requested level and source_type")
    if any(
        item.source_type is not resolved_source
        or (source and item.period != source[0].period)
        for item in centers
    ):
        raise ValueError("zoushis must consume matching official zhongshus")
    if any(
        right.start_index <= left.start_index
        for left, right in zip(centers, centers[1:], strict=False)
    ):
        raise ValueError("official zhongshus must be ordered by start_index")

    inferred_modes = {item.mode for item in centers}
    if resolved_mode is not None and any(item.mode is not resolved_mode for item in centers):
        raise ValueError("official zhongshus do not match the requested mode")
    if resolved_mode is None and len(inferred_modes) == 1:
        resolved_mode = next(iter(inferred_modes))

    if resolved_mode is ZhongShuMode.SAME_LEVEL:
        # 兼容纯函数入口会重放同一状态机，但只用于验证调用方传入的正式
        # 中枢逐对象完全一致；不一致必须显式拒绝，绝不静默替换。Engine
        # 正式路径直接缓存同一个 LevelBuildResult，不会走这里二次扫描。
        canonical = build_same_level_result(
            source,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        )
        if canonical.zhongshus != tuple(centers):
            raise ValueError(
                "same-level official zhongshus do not match the shared state result"
            )
        return canonical

    all_zoushis: list[CompletedZouShi] = []
    same_level_mode = resolved_mode is ZhongShuMode.SAME_LEVEL
    center_index = 0
    run_start = 0
    for index in range(1, len(source) + 1):
        if index < len(source) and _level_continuous(source[index - 1], source[index]):
            continue
        run = source[run_start:index]
        run_centers: list[ZhongShu] = []
        run_center_positions: list[int] = []
        if run:
            first_source_index = run[0].source_index
            last_source_index = run[-1].source_index
            while (
                center_index < len(centers)
                and centers[center_index].start_index <= last_source_index
            ):
                center = centers[center_index]
                if (
                    center.start_index < first_source_index
                    or center.end_index > last_source_index
                ):
                    raise ValueError("official zhongshu crosses a discontinuous leg run")
                run_center_positions.append(center_index)
                run_centers.append(center)
                center_index += 1
        run_zoushis = _build_zoushis_for_run(
            run,
            run_centers,
            run_center_positions,
            profile_id=profile_id,
            config_hash=config_hash,
        )
        for left, right in zip(run_zoushis, run_zoushis[1:], strict=False):
            if (
                not same_level_mode
                and left.zhongshu_ids[-1] != right.zhongshu_ids[0]
            ):
                raise ValueError("adjacent zoushis must share one boundary zhongshu")
            if (left.end.ts, left.end.price) != (right.start.ts, right.start.price):
                raise ValueError("adjacent zoushis must share one boundary coordinate")
        all_zoushis.extend(run_zoushis)
        run_start = index
    if center_index != len(centers):
        raise ValueError("official zhongshu is outside the supplied leg prefix")
    all_zoushis.sort(key=lambda item: (item.start.source_index, item.end.source_index, item.id))
    return LevelBuildResult(
        level=resolved_level,
        source_type=resolved_source,
        legs=tuple(source),
        zhongshus=tuple(centers),
        zoushis=tuple(all_zoushis),
    )


def build_l0_zoushis(
    bis: Sequence[Bi],
    n_merged: int,
    zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    *,
    period: str,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> LevelBuildResult:
    """把最长 confirmed 笔前缀适配为 L0，并消费中枢层正式结果。"""
    legs: list[LevelLeg] = []
    for index, (bi, confirmed) in enumerate(
        zip(bis, bi_confirmed_flags(bis, n_merged), strict=True)
    ):
        if not confirmed:
            break
        legs.append(
            LevelLeg(
                period=period,
                level=ZouShiLevel.L0,
                source_type=ZhongShuSource.BI,
                source_id=bi.id,
                source_index=index,
                direction=bi.direction,
                start_ts=bi.start_ts,
                end_ts=bi.end_ts,
                start_price=bi.start_price,
                end_price=bi.end_price,
                high=bi.high,
                low=bi.low,
                trace=bi.trace,
            )
        )
    return build_zoushis_from_zhongshus(
        legs,
        zhongshus,
        level=ZouShiLevel.L0,
        source_type=ZhongShuSource.BI,
        profile_id=profile_id,
        config_hash=config_hash,
    )


def build_l1_zoushis(
    xianduans: Sequence[Xianduan],
    zhongshus: Sequence[ZhongShu],
    *,
    cfg: ChanConfig,
    period: str,
    profile_id: str,
    config_hash: str | None,
) -> LevelBuildResult:
    """把 standard Engine 的最长 frozen 线段前缀适配为 L1。"""
    selected = select_l1_frozen_xianduan_prefix(xianduans, cfg)
    legs = [
        LevelLeg(
            period=period,
            level=ZouShiLevel.L1,
            source_type=ZhongShuSource.XIANDUAN,
            source_id=item.id,
            source_index=index,
            direction=item.direction,
            start_ts=item.start_ts,
            end_ts=item.end_ts,
            start_price=item.start_price,
            end_price=item.end_price,
            high=item.high,
            low=item.low,
            trace=item.trace,
        )
        for index, item in enumerate(selected)
    ]
    return build_zoushis_from_zhongshus(
        legs,
        zhongshus,
        level=ZouShiLevel.L1,
        source_type=ZhongShuSource.XIANDUAN,
        profile_id=profile_id,
        config_hash=config_hash,
    )

检查什么：5个走势全部字段、共享边界、真实极值端点、成员与中枢ID、冻结状态、关闭证据及最新20/4/2。

手工创建：check_05_trend.py
from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import enum
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

from manual_rebuild.pipeline import build, load_csv

FIXTURE = Path("fixtures/dce-m-main-15m.csv")
EXPECTED_DIGEST = "62860e34705a4d21b521113162139d23e2da29af579586be6be6a46cccb88588"

def normalize(value: object) -> object:
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value):
        return {field.name: normalize(getattr(value, field.name)) for field in dataclasses.fields(value)}
    if isinstance(value, enum.Enum):
        return value.value
    if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
        return [normalize(item) for item in value]
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(key): normalize(item) for key, item in sorted(value.items(), key=lambda pair: str(pair[0]))}
    return value


def object_digest(items: object) -> str:
    rows = [{"id": item.id, "fields": normalize(item)} for item in items]
    payload = json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
    return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def main() -> None:
    result = build(load_csv(FIXTURE))
    trends = result.trends
    assert len(trends) == 5
    assert [item.completion_state.value for item in trends] == ["frozen", "frozen", "frozen", "frozen", "forming"]
    for left, right in zip(trends, trends[1:]):
        assert (left.end.ts, left.end.price) == (right.start.ts, right.start.price)
    latest = trends[-1]
    members = set(latest.source_member_ids)
    tails = [item for item in result.segments if item.id not in members and item.start_ts >= latest.end.ts]
    assert (len(latest.source_member_ids), len(latest.zhongshu_ids), len(tails)) == (20, 4, 2)
    actual = object_digest(trends)
    assert actual == EXPECTED_DIGEST, (EXPECTED_DIGEST, actual)
    print("check_05_trend PASS: 5 trends, latest 20 members / 4 centers / 2 tails", actual)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_05_trend.py
预期：check_05_trend PASS: 5 trends, latest 20 members / 4 centers / 2 tails

最终图形契约

最终图像不靠手画：同对象必须得到同一SVG字节

算法一致只解决“算出了什么”；渲染器再固定“怎样画”。两者同时通过，不同复刻者才能得到同一对象和同一最终图。

唯一标准：figures/26-final-standard.svg。图像肉眼核对只用于发现明显漏层或错色；正式验收必须比较整个文件SHA-256，不能以“看起来差不多”代替。输入CSV、算法对象或序列化规则任一变化，哈希都会变化。

固定规则

  1. 算法仍读取完整10,060根CSV并从头建立状态；主图只把代表性raw[2427:2677]裁成共同展示窗口，不得把这250根误当成独立算法输入。
  2. 七层累计主图统一为1320×680像素的单一窗口，不分屏、不需要横向拖动；时间固定为2025-04-07 09:00～2025-04-21 22:00。
  3. 七图使用完全相同的横轴、纵轴、250根K线和成交量；后一层只允许新增本层对象。
  4. 绘制顺序固定为原始K线与成交量→合并K线→分型→笔→线段→中枢→走势。
  5. 最终结果与窗口相交的对象固定为169根合并K线、88个分型、14条笔、5条已冻结线段、1个正式段中枢和2个已冻结走势。
  6. 该窗口是完整数据计算完成后的回看裁切，不是2025-04-21 22:00当时的实时快照；图中只看到进入线段E的尾部、完整核心A/B/C和第一条延伸的起段，E起点、第一条延伸终点、其余延伸链及关闭证据位于窗口外。
  7. 属性顺序、小数两位、换行、颜色、虚实线和透明度全部由渲染器固定；不写随机ID、生成时间或绝对路径。
  8. 可见字符使用内置5×7矢量路径，SVG中禁止<text>，不依赖系统字体。
  9. 形成中或待确认结构只能用虚线；实线不得表达尚未冻结的结构。

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import html
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable, Sequence

from manual_rebuild.data import Kline

FONT: dict[str, Sequence[str]] = {' ': ('00000', '00000', '00000', '00000', '00000', '00000', '00000'), '0': ('01110', '10001', '10011', '10101', '11001', '10001', '01110'), '1': ('00100', '01100', def f(value: float) -> str:
    return f"{value:.2f}"


class Svg:
    def __init__(self, title: str, width: int = 1400, height: int = 560) -> None:
        self.width = width
        self.height = height
        self.parts = [
            f'<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="{width}" height="{height}" viewBox="0 0 {width} {height}" role="img" aria-label="{html.escape(title, quote=True)}">',
            f'<path d="M0 0H{width}V{height}H0Z" fill="#f8fafc" stroke="none"/>',
        ]

    def path(self, d: str, fill: str = "none", stroke: str = "none", width: float = 1.0, dash: str | None = None, opacity: float = 1.0) -> None:
        attrs = [f'd="{d}"', f'fill="{fill}"', f'stroke="{stroke}"', f'stroke-width="{f(width)}"']
        if dash is not None:
            attrs.append(f'stroke-dasharray="{dash}"')
        attrs.append(f'opacity="{f(opacity)}"')
        self.parts.append("<path " + " ".join(attrs) + "/>")

    def rect(self, x: float, y: float, w: float, h: float, fill: str, stroke: str = "none", width: float = 1.0, opacity: float = 1.0) -> None:
        self.path(f"M{f(x)} {f(y)}H{f(x+w)}V{f(y+h)}H{f(x)}Z", fill, stroke, width, opacity=opacity)

    def line(self, x1: float, y1: float, x2: float, y2: float, color: str, width: float = 1.0, dash: str | None = None, opacity: float = 1.0) -> None:
        self.path(f"M{f(x1)} {f(y1)}L{f(x2)} {f(y2)}", "none", color, width, dash, opacity)

    def label(self, value: object, x: float, y: float, color: str = "#475569", scale: float = 1.25) -> None:
        cursor = x
        commands: list[str] = []
        for char in str(value).upper():
            glyph = FONT.get(char, FONT[" "])
            for row, bits in enumerate(glyph):
                for column, bit in enumerate(bits):
                    if bit == "1":
                        px = cursor + column * scale
                        py = y + row * scale
                        commands.append(f"M{f(px)} {f(py)}h{f(scale)}v{f(scale)}h-{f(scale)}Z")
            cursor += 6 * scale
        if commands:
            self.path("".join(commands), color, "none", 0.0)

    def finish(self) -> str:
        return "\n".join((*self.parts, "</svg>")) + "\n"


def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def sha256_path(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def scale_domain(values: Iterable[float], low: float, high: float) -> Callable[[float], float]:
    seq = list(values)
    minimum = min(seq)
    maximum = max(seq)
    padding = max((maximum - minimum) * 0.08, 1e-9)
    minimum -= padding
    maximum += padding
    return lambda value: high - (value - minimum) / (maximum - minimum) * (high - low)


def x_for(ts: int, start: int, end: int, left: float = 110, right: float = 1330) -> float:
    if end == start:
        return (left + right) / 2
    return left + (ts - start) / (end - start) * (right - left)


def draw_grid(svg: Svg) -> None:
    for i in range(6):
        y = 70 + i * 75
        svg.line(90, y, 1350, y, "#dbe4ea", 1)


def price_scale(svg: Svg, values: Iterable[float]) -> Callable[[float], float]:
    seq = list(values)
    minimum = min(seq)
    maximum = max(seq)
    padding = max((maximum - minimum) * 0.08, 1e-9)
    minimum -= padding
    maximum += padding
    for index in range(6):
        price = maximum - (maximum - minimum) * index / 5
        svg.label(f"{price:.2f}", 12, 64 + index * 75, "#64748b", 0.75)
    return scale_domain(seq, 70, 445)


def sequence_x_mapper(items: Sequence[Kline]) -> Callable[[int], float]:
    timestamps = [item.timestamp for item in items]

    def map_timestamp(timestamp: int) -> float:
        if timestamp <= timestamps[0]:
            return 110.0
        if timestamp >= timestamps[-1]:
            return 1330.0
        for index, (left_ts, right_ts) in enumerate(zip(timestamps, timestamps[1:])):
            if left_ts <= timestamp <= right_ts:
                fraction = (timestamp - left_ts) / (right_ts - left_ts)
                position = index + fraction
                return float(110 + position / (len(timestamps) - 1) * 1220)
        raise ValueError("时间戳超出K线序列")

    return map_timestamp


def draw_klines(svg: Svg, items: Sequence[Kline], *, annotate_rows: bool = False) -> Callable[[float], float] | None:
    if not items:
        return None
    start, end = items[0].timestamp, items[-1].timestamp
    y = price_scale(svg, (value for item in items for value in (item.high, item.low)))
    spacing = 1220 / max(len(items) - 1, 1)
    body = max(6.0, min(26.0, spacing * 0.62))
    for index, item in enumerate(items):
        x = 110 + index / max(len(items) - 1, 1) * 1220
        color = "#ef4444" if item.close >= item.open else "#22c55e"
        svg.line(x, y(item.high), x, y(item.low), color, 2.0)
        top = y(max(item.open, item.close))
        bottom = y(min(item.open, item.close))
        svg.rect(x - body / 2, top, body, max(bottom - top, 2.0), color)
        if annotate_rows and index % max(1, len(items) // 8) == 0:
            svg.label(index + 2, x - 10, 465, "#64748b", 0.85)
    svg.label(datetime.fromtimestamp(start, UTC).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"), 110, 24, "#64748b", 0.9)
    svg.label(datetime.fromtimestamp(end, UTC).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"), 1060, 24, "#64748b", 0.9)
    return y


def draw_line_objects(svg: Svg, items: Sequence[Any], start: int, end: int, price_map: Any, color: str, width: float, *, dashed_forming: bool = False, x_mapper: Any | None = None) -> None:
    for item in items:
        start_ts = int(getattr(item, "start_ts", item.start.ts if hasattr(item, "start") else 0))
        end_ts = int(getattr(item, "end_ts", item.end.ts if hasattr(item, "end") else 0))
        start_price = float(getattr(item, "start_price", item.start.price if hasattr(item, "start") else 0))
        end_price = float(getattr(item, "end_price", item.end.price if hasattr(item, "end") else 0))
        state = getattr(getattr(item, "completion_state", None), "value", None)
        trace = getattr(item, "trace", None)
        freeze = getattr(trace, "freeze_state", None)
        forming = state == "forming" or freeze not in (None, "frozen", "soft_n0")
        start_x = x_mapper(start_ts) if x_mapper is not None else x_for(start_ts, start, end)
        end_x = x_mapper(end_ts) if x_mapper is not None else x_for(end_ts, start, end)
        svg.line(
            start_x,
            price_map(start_price),
            end_x,
            price_map(end_price),
            color,
            width,
            "7.00 5.00" if dashed_forming and forming else None,
        )


def draw_centers(svg: Svg, centers: Sequence[Any], start: int, end: int, price_map: Any, *, x_mapper: Any | None = None) -> None:
    for center in centers:
        x1 = x_mapper(center.start_ts) if x_mapper is not None else x_for(center.start_ts, start, end)
        x2 = x_mapper(center.end_ts) if x_mapper is not None else x_for(center.end_ts, start, end)
        y1 = price_map(center.zg)
        y2 = price_map(center.zd)
        svg.rect(x1, min(y1, y2), max(x2 - x1, 2), abs(y2 - y1), "#f59e0b", "#fbbf24", 1.2, 0.25)

manual_rebuild/canonical_renderer.py

文件用途谁会调用接收什么交出什么明确不负责
把六层对象映射到累计主图和规则图检查关卡6CSV和六层结果39幅规范SVG不修改算法对象
手工创建：manual_rebuild/canonical_renderer.py

from manual_rebuild.svg_base import Svg, f

def sha256_bytes(value: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()


def beijing_text(timestamp: int) -> str:
    from datetime import datetime

    return datetime.fromtimestamp(timestamp, BEIJING).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")


def chart_x(
    timestamp: int,
    all_timestamps: Sequence[int],
    start_index: int,
    pitch: float,
    left: float,
) -> float:
    position = bisect.bisect_left(all_timestamps, timestamp)
    if position >= len(all_timestamps):
        position = len(all_timestamps) - 1
    elif all_timestamps[position] != timestamp and position > 0:
        before = all_timestamps[position - 1]
        after = all_timestamps[position]
        fraction = (timestamp - before) / (after - before)
        return left + ((position - 1 + fraction) - start_index) * pitch
    return left + (position - start_index) * pitch


def draw_market_chart(
    svg: Svg,
    raw: Sequence[Any],
    *,
    global_start: int,
    pitch: float,
    full_raw: Sequence[Any],
) -> tuple[Callable[[int], float], Callable[[float], float]]:
    top = 70.0
    price_bottom = 500.0
    volume_top = 540.0
    volume_bottom = 630.0
    left = 100.0
    right = left + max(len(raw) - 1, 1) * pitch
    prices = [value for item in raw for value in (item.high, item.low)]
    minimum, maximum = min(prices), max(prices)
    padding = max((maximum - minimum) * 0.06, 1e-9)
    minimum -= padding
    maximum += padding

    def y(value: float) -> float:
        return price_bottom - (value - minimum) / (maximum - minimum) * (
            price_bottom - top
        )

    for row in range(6):
        gy = top + row * (price_bottom - top) / 5
        svg.line(90, gy, right + 20, gy, "#dbe4ea", 1)
        price = maximum - row * (maximum - minimum) / 5
        svg.label(f"{price:.2f}", 10, gy - 5, "#64748b", 0.72)

    up_wicks: list[str] = []
    down_wicks: list[str] = []
    up_bodies: list[str] = []
    down_bodies: list[str] = []
    up_volume: list[str] = []
    down_volume: list[str] = []
    max_volume = max(item.volume for item in raw) or 1.0
    body_width = max(5.0, min(8.0, pitch * 0.65))
    wick_width = max(1.2, min(2.0, pitch * 0.18))
    for offset, item in enumerate(raw):
        px = left + offset * pitch
        target_wicks = up_wicks if item.close >= item.open else down_wicks
        target_bodies = up_bodies if item.close >= item.open else down_bodies
        target_volume = up_volume if item.close >= item.open else down_volume
        target_wicks.append(
            f"M{f(px-wick_width/2)} {f(y(item.high))}H{f(px+wick_width/2)}"
            f"V{f(y(item.low))}H{f(px-wick_width/2)}Z"
        )
        body_top = y(max(item.open, item.close))
        body_bottom = max(y(min(item.open, item.close)), body_top + 1.5)
        target_bodies.append(
            f"M{f(px-body_width/2)} {f(body_top)}H{f(px+body_width/2)}"
            f"V{f(body_bottom)}H{f(px-body_width/2)}Z"
        )
        volume_height = item.volume / max_volume * (volume_bottom - volume_top)
        target_volume.append(
            f"M{f(px-body_width/2)} {f(volume_bottom-volume_height)}"
            f"H{f(px+body_width/2)}V{f(volume_bottom)}H{f(px-body_width/2)}Z"
        )
    svg.path("".join(up_wicks + up_bodies), "#ef4444", "none", 0)
    svg.path("".join(down_wicks + down_bodies), "#22c55e", "none", 0)
    svg.path("".join(up_volume), "#ef4444", "none", 0, opacity=0.38)
    svg.path("".join(down_volume), "#22c55e", "none", 0, opacity=0.38)
    svg.line(90, volume_top - 8, right + 20, volume_top - 8, "#cbd5e1", 1)
    svg.label(f"RAW {global_start}-{global_start+len(raw)-1}", 100, 650, "#475569", 0.82)
    svg.label(f"{beijing_text(raw[0].timestamp)} ({raw[0].timestamp})", 100, 24, "#64748b", 0.78)
    svg.label(
        f"{beijing_text(raw[-1].timestamp)} ({raw[-1].timestamp})",
        max(100.0, right - 460),
        24,
        "#64748b",
        0.78,
    )
    timestamps = [item.timestamp for item in full_raw]

    def x(timestamp: int) -> float:
        return chart_x(timestamp, timestamps, global_start, pitch, left)

    return x, y


def draw_merged(
    svg: Svg,
    merged: Sequence[Any],
    x: Callable[[int], float],
    y: Callable[[float], float],
    start: int,
    end: int,
) -> None:
    commands: list[str] = []
    for item in merged:
        if item.end_index < start or item.start_index > end:
            continue
        x1 = x(item.start_ts) - 3
        x2 = x(item.end_ts) + 3
        commands.append(
            f"M{f(x1)} {f(y(item.high))}H{f(x2)}V{f(y(item.low))}H{f(x1)}Z"
        )
    svg.path("".join(commands), "none", "#0284c7", 0.8, opacity=0.72)


def draw_fractals(
    svg: Svg,
    fractals: Sequence[Any],
    x: Callable[[int], float],
    y: Callable[[float], float],
) -> None:
    tops: list[str] = []
    bottoms: list[str] = []
    for item in fractals:
        price = item.high if item.fx_type.value == "top" else item.low
        px = x(item.timestamp)
        py = y(price)
        command = (
            f"M{f(px)} {f(py-6)}L{f(px-5)} {f(py+4)}L{f(px+5)} {f(py+4)}Z"
            if item.fx_type.value == "top"
            else f"M{f(px)} {f(py+6)}L{f(px-5)} {f(py-4)}L{f(px+5)} {f(py-4)}Z"
        )
        (tops if item.fx_type.value == "top" else bottoms).append(command)
    svg.path("".join(tops), "#e11d48", "none", 0)
    svg.path("".join(bottoms), "#059669", "none", 0)


def draw_strokes(
    svg: Svg,
    strokes: Sequence[Any],
    flags: Sequence[bool],
    x: Callable[[int], float],
    y: Callable[[float], float],
) -> None:
    confirmed: list[str] = []
    forming: list[str] = []
    for item, flag in zip(strokes, flags, strict=True):
        command = (
            f"M{f(x(item.start_ts))} {f(y(item.start_price))}"
            f"L{f(x(item.end_ts))} {f(y(item.end_price))}"
        )
        (confirmed if flag else forming).append(command)
    svg.path("".join(confirmed), "none", "#d97706", 2.4)
    svg.path("".join(forming), "none", "#f59e0b", 2.4, "8.00 6.00")


def draw_segments(
    svg: Svg,
    segments: Sequence[Any],
    x: Callable[[int], float],
    y: Callable[[float], float],
) -> None:
    frozen: list[str] = []
    provisional: list[str] = []
    for item in segments:
        command = (
            f"M{f(x(item.start_ts))} {f(y(item.start_price))}"
            f"L{f(x(item.end_ts))} {f(y(item.end_price))}"
        )
        freeze = getattr(item.trace, "freeze_state", None)
        (frozen if freeze == "frozen" else provisional).append(command)
    svg.path("".join(frozen), "none", "#0284c7", 3.2)
    svg.path("".join(provisional), "none", "#38bdf8", 3.2, "9.00 6.00")


def draw_centers(
    svg: Svg,
    centers: Sequence[Any],
    x: Callable[[int], float],
    y: Callable[[float], float],
) -> None:
    for item in centers:
        x1 = x(item.start_ts)
        x2 = x(item.end_ts)
        y1 = y(item.zg)
        y2 = y(item.zd)
        dash = "7.00 5.00" if item.extension_state.value == "open" else None
        svg.path(
            f"M{f(x1)} {f(y1)}H{f(x2)}V{f(y2)}H{f(x1)}Z",
            "#a855f7",
            "#7e22ce",
            1.5,
            dash,
            0.14,
        )


def draw_trends(
    svg: Svg,
    trends: Sequence[Any],
    x: Callable[[int], float],
    y: Callable[[float], float],
) -> None:
    frozen: list[str] = []
    forming: list[str] = []
    for item in trends:
        command = (
            f"M{f(x(item.start.ts))} {f(y(item.start.price))}"
            f"L{f(x(item.end.ts))} {f(y(item.end.price))}"
        )
        (forming if item.completion_state.value == "forming" else frozen).append(command)
    svg.path("".join(frozen), "none", "#e11d48", 4.4)
    svg.path("".join(forming), "none", "#fb7185", 4.4, "11.00 7.00")


def panel_objects(
    result: Result,
    start: int,
    end: int,
) -> tuple[list[Any], list[Any], list[Any], list[Any], list[Any], list[Any]]:
    """只选择与当前固定分屏相交的对象，不改变对象本身。"""

    panel_raw = result.raw[start : end + 1]
    first_ts = panel_raw[0].timestamp
    last_ts = panel_raw[-1].timestamp
    merged = [
        item
        for item in result.merged
        if not (item.end_index < start or item.start_index > end)
    ]
    fractals = [
        item for item in result.fractals if first_ts <= item.timestamp <= last_ts
    ]
    strokes = [
        item
        for item in result.strokes
        if not (item.end_ts < first_ts or item.start_ts > last_ts)
    ]
    segments = [
        item
        for item in result.segments
        if not (item.end_ts < first_ts or item.start_ts > last_ts)
    ]
    centers = [
        item
        for item in result.centers
        if not (item.end_ts < first_ts or item.start_ts > last_ts)
    ]
    trends = [
        item
        for item in result.trends
        if not (item.end.ts < first_ts or item.start.ts > last_ts)
    ]
    return merged, fractals, strokes, segments, centers, trends


def draw_layer_objects(
    svg: Svg,
    result: Result,
    *,
    layer: int,
    start: int,
    end: int,
    x: Callable[[int], float],
    y: Callable[[float], float],
    flag_by_id: dict[str, bool],
) -> None:
    """按固定顺序累计叠加；低层绝不提前绘制高层对象。"""

    merged, fractals, strokes, segments, centers, trends = panel_objects(
        result, start, end
    )
    if layer >= 1:
        draw_merged(svg, merged, x, y, start, end)
    if layer >= 2:
        draw_fractals(svg, fractals, x, y)
    if layer >= 3:
        draw_strokes(
            svg,
            strokes,
            [flag_by_id[item.id] for item in strokes],
            x,
            y,
        )
    if layer >= 4:
        draw_segments(svg, segments, x, y)
    if layer >= 5:
        draw_centers(svg, centers, x, y)
    if layer >= 6:
        draw_trends(svg, trends, x, y)


def shared_svg(result: Result, layer: int, title: str) -> str:
    raw = result.raw[WINDOW_START : WINDOW_END + 1]
    svg = Svg(title, SHARED_WIDTH, HEIGHT)
    pitch = (SHARED_WIDTH - 140.0) / max(len(raw) - 1, 1)
    x, y = draw_market_chart(
        svg,
        raw,
        global_start=WINDOW_START,
        pitch=pitch,
        full_raw=result.raw,
    )
    all_flags = bi_confirmed_flags(result.strokes, len(result.merged))
    flag_by_id = {
        item.id: flag
        for item, flag in zip(result.strokes, all_flags, strict=True)
    }
    draw_layer_objects(
        svg,
        result,
        layer=layer,
        start=WINDOW_START,
        end=WINDOW_END,
        x=x,
        y=y,
        flag_by_id=flag_by_id,
    )
    svg.label(
        f"REPRESENTATIVE 250 LAYER {layer}",
        SHARED_WIDTH - 350,
        650,
        "#334155",
        0.72,
    )
    return svg.finish()


def raw_bounds(result: Result, start_ts: int, end_ts: int, pad: int = 8) -> tuple[int, int]:
    timestamps = [item.timestamp for item in result.raw]
    start = max(0, bisect.bisect_left(timestamps, start_ts) - pad)
    end = min(len(result.raw) - 1, bisect.bisect_right(timestamps, end_ts) - 1 + pad)
    return start, end


def local_svg(
    result: Result,
    *,
    title: str,
    start_ts: int,
    end_ts: int,
    merged: Sequence[Any] = (),
    fractals: Sequence[Any] = (),
    strokes: Sequence[Any] = (),
    segments: Sequence[Any] = (),
    centers: Sequence[Any] = (),
    trends: Sequence[Any] = (),
    highlight_opposite_to: Any | None = None,
) -> str:
    start, end = raw_bounds(result, start_ts, end_ts)
    raw = result.raw[start : end + 1]
    width = int(120 + max(len(raw) - 1, 1) * PITCH)
    svg = Svg(title, width, HEIGHT)
    x, y = draw_market_chart(svg, raw, global_start=start, pitch=PITCH, full_raw=result.raw)
    flags_all = bi_confirmed_flags(result.strokes, len(result.merged))
    flag_by_id = {item.id: flag for item, flag in zip(result.strokes, flags_all, strict=True)}
    if merged:
        draw_merged(svg, merged, x, y, start, end)
    if fractals:
        draw_fractals(svg, fractals, x, y)
    if strokes:
        draw_strokes(svg, strokes, [flag_by_id[item.id] for item in strokes], x, y)
    if highlight_opposite_to is not None:
        opposite = [item for item in strokes if item.direction is not highlight_opposite_to.direction]
        for item in opposite:
            svg.line(x(item.start_ts), y(item.start_price), x(item.end_ts), y(item.end_price), "#2563eb", 5.0)
    if segments:
        draw_segments(svg, segments, x, y)
    if centers:
        draw_centers(svg, centers, x, y)
    if trends:
        draw_trends(svg, trends, x, y)
    return svg.finish()


def merged_for_raw(result: Result, start: int, end: int) -> list[Any]:
    return [
        item
        for item in result.merged
        if not (item.end_index < start or item.start_index > end)
    ]


def fractals_for_merged(result: Result, start: int, end: int) -> list[Any]:
    return [item for item in result.fractals if start <= item.index <= end]


def actual_window_svg(
    result: Result,
    *,
    title: str,
    raw_start: int,
    raw_end: int,
    merged: Sequence[Any] = (),
    fractals: Sequence[Any] = (),
    strokes: Sequence[Any] = (),
    segments: Sequence[Any] = (),
    centers: Sequence[Any] = (),
    trends: Sequence[Any] = (),
    overlap_band: tuple[int, int, float, float] | None = None,
    color_segments: bool = False,
    pitch: float = PITCH,
) -> str:
    """按真实原始下标画局部窗口；所有覆盖对象仍来自完整批量结果。"""

    raw_start = max(0, raw_start)
    raw_end = min(len(result.raw) - 1, raw_end)
    raw = result.raw[raw_start : raw_end + 1]
    width = int(120 + max(len(raw) - 1, 1) * pitch)
    svg = Svg(title, width, HEIGHT)
    x, y = draw_market_chart(
        svg,
        raw,
        global_start=raw_start,
        pitch=pitch,
        full_raw=result.raw,
    )
    flags_all = bi_confirmed_flags(result.strokes, len(result.merged))
    flag_by_id = {
        item.id: flag
        for item, flag in zip(result.strokes, flags_all, strict=True)
    }
    if merged:
        draw_merged(svg, merged, x, y, raw_start, raw_end)
    if fractals:
        draw_fractals(svg, fractals, x, y)
    if strokes:
        draw_strokes(svg, strokes, [flag_by_id[item.id] for item in strokes], x, y)
    if segments:
        draw_segments(svg, segments, x, y)
        if color_segments:
            palette = ("#7c3aed", "#2563eb", "#0891b2", "#db2777")
            for index, item in enumerate(segments):
                svg.line(
                    x(item.start_ts),
                    y(item.start_price),
                    x(item.end_ts),
                    y(item.end_price),
                    palette[index % len(palette)],
                    4.8,
                )
    if overlap_band is not None:
        band_start, band_end, low, high = overlap_band
        if low <= high:
            if low == high:
                svg.line(x(band_start), y(low), x(band_end), y(high), "#f97316", 4.0)
            else:
                svg.rect(
                    x(band_start),
                    y(high),
                    max(x(band_end) - x(band_start), 2.0),
                    max(y(low) - y(high), 2.0),
                    "#f59e0b",
                    "#c2410c",
                    1.5,
                    0.18,
                )
    if centers:
        draw_centers(svg, centers, x, y)
    if trends:
        draw_trends(svg, trends, x, y)
    return svg.finish()


def raw_index_bounds(result: Result, items: Sequence[Any], pad: int = 8) -> tuple[int, int]:
    def start_value(item: Any) -> int:
        if hasattr(item, "start_ts"):
            return int(item.start_ts)
        if hasattr(item, "start"):
            return int(item.start.ts)
        return int(item.timestamp)

    def end_value(item: Any) -> int:
        if hasattr(item, "end_ts"):
            return int(item.end_ts)
        if hasattr(item, "end"):
            return int(item.end.ts)
        return int(item.timestamp)

    start_ts = min(start_value(item) for item in items)
    end_ts = max(end_value(item) for item in items)
    return raw_bounds(result, start_ts, end_ts, pad)


def member_strokes(result: Result, segment: Any) -> list[Any]:
    return [
        item
        for item in result.strokes
        if item.start_ts >= segment.start_ts and item.end_ts <= segment.end_ts
    ]


def build_rule_svgs(result: Result, down_result: Result) -> dict[str, str]:
    """为定义与边界章节生成真实正反例；不创建任何手画行情路径。"""

    # 包含：四个局部案例均来自主CSV完整结果对应的真实原始下标。
    merge_current = merged_for_raw(result, 2425, 2432)
    merge_new = merged_for_raw(result, 2434, 2440)
    merge_direction = merged_for_raw(result, 2430, 2441)
    merge_equal = merged_for_raw(result, 2528, 2534)
    merge_lead = merged_for_raw(result, 0, 16)
    tail_start = len(result.raw) - 32
    merge_tail = merged_for_raw(result, tail_start, len(result.raw) - 1)

    # 分型：同一真实序列中，底、顶和滑窗继续扫描三个视角。
    fx_slice = result.merged[3838:3846]
    fx_raw_start = min(item.start_index for item in fx_slice) - 6
    fx_raw_end = max(item.end_index for item in fx_slice) + 6
    top_fx = [item for item in result.fractals if item.index == 3843]
    bottom_fx = [item for item in result.fractals if item.index == 3839]
    slide_fx = fractals_for_merged(result, 3838, 3845)

    # 线段：前三笔重叠成立/不成立、真实成员连续性、无缺口确认和尾部冻结。
    overlap_strokes = result.strokes[0:3]
    reject_strokes = result.strokes[15:18]
    overlap_start, overlap_end = raw_index_bounds(result, overlap_strokes)
    reject_start, reject_end = raw_index_bounds(result, reject_strokes)
    overlap_low = max(item.low for item in overlap_strokes)
    overlap_high = min(item.high for item in overlap_strokes)
    base_segment = result.segments[0]
    base_members = member_strokes(result, base_segment)
    base_start, base_end = raw_index_bounds(result, base_members)
    no_gap_segment = result.segments[51]
    no_gap_members = member_strokes(result, no_gap_segment)
    no_gap_start, no_gap_end = raw_index_bounds(result, no_gap_members)
    tail_segments = result.segments[65:69]
    tail_start_idx, tail_end_idx = raw_index_bounds(result, tail_segments)

    # 中枢：E/A/B/C真实候选与正式中枢对象。
    no_center_legs = result.segments[0:4]
    no_center_start, no_center_end = raw_index_bounds(result, no_center_legs)
    entry_miss_legs = result.segments[1:5]
    entry_miss_start, entry_miss_end = raw_index_bounds(result, entry_miss_legs)
    tangent_legs = result.segments[47:51]
    tangent_start, tangent_end = raw_index_bounds(result, tangent_legs)
    close_up = result.centers[7]
    close_up_legs = result.segments[
        close_up.entry_evidence_index : (close_up.close_evidence_index or close_up.end_index) + 1
    ]
    close_up_start, close_up_end = raw_index_bounds(result, close_up_legs)
    close_down = result.centers[2]
    close_down_legs = result.segments[
        close_down.entry_evidence_index : (close_down.close_evidence_index or close_down.end_index) + 1
    ]
    close_down_start, close_down_end = raw_index_bounds(result, close_down_legs)
    open_center = result.centers[8]
    open_legs = result.segments[open_center.entry_evidence_index : open_center.end_index + 1]
    open_start, open_end = raw_index_bounds(result, open_legs)

    # 走势：前缀结果用来还原“一个中枢→两个中枢”的真实生长截面。
    one_center_result = build(result.raw[:1934])
    up_result = build(result.raw[:2751])
    one_center_items = list(one_center_result.segments) + list(one_center_result.centers)
    one_start, one_end = raw_index_bounds(one_center_result, one_center_items)
    up_items = list(up_result.segments) + list(up_result.centers)
    up_start, up_end = raw_index_bounds(up_result, up_items)
    down_centers = down_result.centers[1:3]
    down_segments = down_result.segments[
        down_centers[0].entry_evidence_index : (down_centers[-1].close_evidence_index or down_centers[-1].end_index) + 1
    ]
    down_start, down_end = raw_index_bounds(down_result, down_segments)
    overlap_centers = result.centers[1:3]
    overlap_segments = result.segments[
        overlap_centers[0].entry_evidence_index : (overlap_centers[-1].close_evidence_index or overlap_centers[-1].end_index) + 1
    ]
    relation_start, relation_end = raw_index_bounds(result, overlap_segments)
    first_trend = result.trends[0]
    first_members = [item for item in result.segments if item.id in first_trend.source_member_ids]
    extreme_start, extreme_end = raw_index_bounds(result, first_members)
    latest_trend = result.trends[-1]
    latest_members = [item for item in result.segments if item.id in latest_trend.source_member_ids]
    excluded_tail = result.segments[len(latest_members) + result.segments.index(latest_members[0]) :]
    tail_relation_items = latest_members + list(excluded_tail)
    latest_start, latest_end = raw_index_bounds(result, tail_relation_items)

    return {
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[0]: actual_window_svg(result, title="current contains new", raw_start=2428, raw_end=2435, merged=merge_current),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[1]: actual_window_svg(result, title="new contains current", raw_start=2432, raw_end=2443, merged=merge_new),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[2]: actual_window_svg(result, title="direction after non containment", raw_start=2429, raw_end=2443, merged=merge_direction),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[3]: actual_window_svg(result, title="equal boundary is containment", raw_start=2526, raw_end=2536, merged=merge_equal),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[4]: actual_window_svg(result, title="lead buffer establishes direction", raw_start=0, raw_end=16, merged=merge_lead),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[5]: actual_window_svg(result, title="tail append keeps stable prefix", raw_start=tail_start, raw_end=len(result.raw) - 1, merged=merge_tail),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[6]: actual_window_svg(result, title="three merged bars top fractal", raw_start=fx_raw_start, raw_end=fx_raw_end, merged=fx_slice, fractals=top_fx),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[7]: actual_window_svg(result, title="three merged bars bottom fractal", raw_start=fx_raw_start, raw_end=fx_raw_end, merged=fx_slice, fractals=bottom_fx),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[8]: actual_window_svg(result, title="fractal sliding window", raw_start=fx_raw_start, raw_end=fx_raw_end, merged=fx_slice, fractals=slide_fx),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[9]: actual_window_svg(result, title="segment first three strokes overlap", raw_start=overlap_start, raw_end=overlap_end, strokes=overlap_strokes, overlap_band=(overlap_strokes[0].start_ts, overlap_strokes[-1].end_ts, overlap_low, overlap_high)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[10]: actual_window_svg(result, title="segment first three strokes no overlap", raw_start=reject_start, raw_end=reject_end, strokes=reject_strokes),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[11]: actual_window_svg(result, title="segment odd continuous members", raw_start=base_start, raw_end=base_end, strokes=base_members, segments=(base_segment,)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[12]: actual_window_svg(result, title="segment no gap feature confirmation", raw_start=no_gap_start, raw_end=no_gap_end, strokes=no_gap_members, segments=(no_gap_segment,)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[13]: actual_window_svg(result, title="segment candidate then frozen", raw_start=tail_start_idx, raw_end=tail_end_idx, strokes=[item for item in result.strokes if tail_segments[0].start_ts <= item.start_ts <= tail_segments[-1].end_ts], segments=tail_segments),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[14]: actual_window_svg(result, title="center core has no triple overlap", raw_start=no_center_start, raw_end=no_center_end, segments=no_center_legs, color_segments=True),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[15]: actual_window_svg(result, title="center entry does not touch core", raw_start=entry_miss_start, raw_end=entry_miss_end, segments=entry_miss_legs, color_segments=True, overlap_band=(entry_miss_legs[1].start_ts, entry_miss_legs[-1].end_ts, 2645.0, 2699.0)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[16]: actual_window_svg(result, title="center tangent overlap is valid", raw_start=tangent_start, raw_end=tangent_end, segments=tangent_legs, color_segments=True, overlap_band=(tangent_legs[1].start_ts, tangent_legs[-1].end_ts, 2809.0, 2809.0)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[17]: actual_window_svg(result, title="center closes upward", raw_start=close_up_start, raw_end=close_up_end, segments=close_up_legs, centers=(close_up,)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[18]: actual_window_svg(result, title="center closes downward", raw_start=close_down_start, raw_end=close_down_end, segments=close_down_legs, centers=(close_down,)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[19]: actual_window_svg(result, title="center remains open at tail", raw_start=open_start, raw_end=open_end, segments=open_legs, centers=(open_center,)),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[20]: actual_window_svg(one_center_result, title="one center forms consolidation", raw_start=one_start, raw_end=one_end, segments=one_center_result.segments, centers=one_center_result.centers, trends=one_center_result.trends, pitch=4.0),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[21]: actual_window_svg(up_result, title="two centers strict upward relation", raw_start=up_start, raw_end=up_end, segments=up_result.segments, centers=up_result.centers, trends=up_result.trends, pitch=4.0),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[22]: actual_window_svg(down_result, title="two centers strict downward relation", raw_start=down_start, raw_end=down_end, segments=down_segments, centers=down_centers, trends=(down_result.trends[-1],), pitch=4.0),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[23]: actual_window_svg(result, title="two centers outer ranges overlap", raw_start=relation_start, raw_end=relation_end, segments=overlap_segments, centers=overlap_centers, pitch=4.0),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[24]: actual_window_svg(result, title="trend endpoint uses real member extreme", raw_start=extreme_start, raw_end=extreme_end, segments=first_members, centers=result.centers[0:3], trends=(first_trend,), pitch=4.0),
        RULE_OUTPUT_NAMES[25]: actual_window_svg(result, title="latest trend excludes two tail segments", raw_start=latest_start, raw_end=latest_end, segments=tail_relation_items, centers=result.centers[5:9], trends=(latest_trend,), pitch=4.0),
    }


def build_svgs(result: Result, down_result: Result) -> dict[str, str]:
    segment_feature = result.segments[51]
    segment_gap = result.segments[49]
    tail_segments = result.segments[65:69]
    center = result.centers[7]
    center_segments = result.segments[center.entry_evidence_index : (center.close_evidence_index or center.end_index) + 1]
    core_segments = result.segments[center.start_index : center.start_index + 3]
    # 图24只还原核心 A/B/C 刚刚成立的历史截面，不能把后来延伸和关闭后的字段倒灌回去。
    established_center = dataclasses.replace(
        center,
        gg=max(item.high for item in core_segments),
        dd=min(item.low for item in core_segments),
        end_ts=center.core_end_ts,
        end_index=center.start_index + 2,
        extended_member_ids=(),
        member_count=3,
        extension_state=type(center.extension_state).OPEN,
        close_evidence_id=None,
        close_evidence_index=None,
    )
    left_trend = result.trends[3]
    right_trend = result.trends[4]
    transition_segments = result.segments[43:52]
    transition_centers = result.centers[5:7]
    output = {
        SHARED_OUTPUT_NAMES[0]: shared_svg(
            result, 0, "canonical layer 0 raw market"
        ),
        SHARED_OUTPUT_NAMES[1]: shared_svg(
            result, 1, "canonical layer 1 merged kline"
        ),
        SHARED_OUTPUT_NAMES[2]: shared_svg(
            result, 2, "canonical layer 2 fractal"
        ),
        SHARED_OUTPUT_NAMES[3]: shared_svg(
            result, 3, "canonical layer 3 stroke"
        ),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[0]: shared_svg(result, 4, "canonical layer 4 segment"),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[1]: local_svg(
            result,
            title="segment feature sequence",
            start_ts=segment_feature.start_ts,
            end_ts=segment_feature.end_ts,
            strokes=member_strokes(result, segment_feature),
            segments=(segment_feature,),
            highlight_opposite_to=segment_feature,
        ),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[2]: local_svg(
            result,
            title="segment gap second sequence",
            start_ts=segment_gap.start_ts,
            end_ts=segment_gap.end_ts,
            strokes=member_strokes(result, segment_gap),
            segments=(segment_gap,),
            highlight_opposite_to=segment_gap,
        ),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[3]: local_svg(
            result,
            title="segment tail states",
            start_ts=tail_segments[0].start_ts,
            end_ts=tail_segments[-1].end_ts,
            strokes=[item for item in result.strokes if item.start_ts >= tail_segments[0].start_ts],
            segments=tail_segments,
        ),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[4]: shared_svg(result, 5, "canonical layer 5 center"),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[5]: local_svg(
            result,
            title="center established",
            start_ts=center_segments[0].start_ts,
            end_ts=result.segments[center.start_index + 2].end_ts,
            segments=center_segments[:4],
            centers=(established_center,),
        ),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[6]: local_svg(
            result,
            title="center extension and close",
            start_ts=center_segments[0].start_ts,
            end_ts=center_segments[-1].end_ts,
            segments=center_segments,
            centers=(center,),
        ),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[7]: shared_svg(result, 6, "canonical final trend standard"),
        OUTPUT_NAMES[8]: local_svg(
            result,
            title="trend freeze and next forming",
            start_ts=transition_segments[0].start_ts,
            end_ts=transition_segments[-1].end_ts,
            segments=transition_segments,
            centers=transition_centers,
            trends=(left_trend, right_trend),
        ),
    }
    output.update(build_rule_svgs(result, down_result))
    return output


def render_all(fixtures: Path, output: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    result = build(load_csv(fixtures / "dce-m-main-15m.csv"))
    down_result = build(load_csv(fixtures / "binance-btcusdt-4h.csv"))
    svgs = build_svgs(result, down_result)
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for filename, svg in svgs.items():
        (output / filename).write_text(svg, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
    return {filename: sha256_bytes(svg.encode("utf-8")) for filename, svg in svgs.items()}


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="生成CSV到走势的固定SVG验收图")
    parser.add_argument("--fixtures", type=Path, default=Path("fixtures"))
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("rendered-svg"))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    first = render_all(args.fixtures, args.output)
    second = render_all(args.fixtures, args.output)
    if first != second:
        raise AssertionError("同一输入连续两次生成的SVG哈希不一致")
    print(json.dumps(second, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

检查关卡6：最终图形是否逐字节一致

检查什么：在两个独立临时目录连续生成全部39幅规范图；逐文件比较SHA-256，并特别锁定最终图。

手工创建：check_06_render.py

def main() -> None:
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as first_dir, tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as second_dir:
        first = render_all(FIXTURES, Path(first_dir))
        second = render_all(FIXTURES, Path(second_dir))
        assert first == second, "连续两次SVG哈希不一致"
        assert first == EXPECTED, (EXPECTED, first)
    print("check_06_render PASS: 39 canonical SVG files, byte-identical", EXPECTED["26-final-standard.svg"])

    main()
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_06_render.py
预期：check_06_render PASS: 39 canonical SVG files, byte-identical
平台边界：Windows已经实测确定性；Linux仍是NOT RUN。因为SVG不依赖字体，预计结构稳定，但在Linux实际运行前不宣称跨平台已经通过。

验收

别人只拿网站包，也能从零得到唯一结果

验收时不给CC源码，只给本教程的HTML、CSS、SVG和CSV；复刻者从空目录按页面手工建立代码，并依次通过七个关卡。

先建立完全相同的运行环境

项目固定值作用
操作系统Windows 11 x64当前字节级确定性实测环境
PythonCPython 3.12.7 x64不要用3.11或3.13代替基线
时区数据tzdata 2026.2Windows的zoneinfo读取Asia/Shanghai所需
文件编码UTF-8，换行LF保证CSV读取和SVG字节一致
额外框架不需要CC、Qlib、AI、数据库或Node.js复现只运行HTML内手写的Python代码
py -3.12 -m venv .venv
.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pip install tzdata==2026.2
.venv\Scripts\python.exe -c "import platform,sys,importlib.metadata as m; print(platform.platform()); print(sys.version); print(m.version('tzdata'))"
环境预期：Python必须显示3.12.7，tzdata必须显示2026.2。操作系统不是Windows时可以研究复现，但本基线只能标记为NOT RUN，不能直接签署字节级通过。

最终应创建的文件

类别文件
公共manual_rebuild/data.py、io.py、pipeline.py
算法algo/trace.py、keys.py、models.py、config.py、combiner.py、fenxing.py、bi.py、xianduan.py、zhongshu_phase.py、same_level.py、zhongshu.py、zoushi_kernel.py、zoushi.py
图形svg_base.py、canonical_renderer.py
检查check_00_csv.py～check_06_render.py

按顺序运行

.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_00_csv.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_01_fractal.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_02_stroke.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_03_segment.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_04_center.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_05_trend.py
.venv\Scripts\python.exe check_06_render.py
层固定结果不能只检查什么
原始K线10,060不能只看CSV能打开
合并K线6,200不能只看数量
分型3,027必须含ID和来源范围
笔502＝501确认＋1形成中必须含14次假反弹处理
线段69＝67冻结＋1准完成＋1待确认必须含状态和证据水位
中枢9＝8关闭＋1开放必须含核心、延伸与关闭证据
走势5＝4冻结＋1形成中必须含共享边界和最新20/4/2
最终SVGe060cfcfd4b5831a939bade3ca3d3a5c54b286b16a86a9b3ec367315f7ed79eb不能只看截图相似

最终产出和哪一张图比较

唯一最终图：复刻者生成的rendered-svg/26-final-standard.svg，必须与教程中的figures/26-final-standard.svg逐字节一致，并得到SHA-256 e060cfcfd4b5831a939bade3ca3d3a5c54b286b16a86a9b3ec367315f7ed79eb。该图展示完整计算结果的代表性250根回看窗口；六层对象是否正确仍由check_00～05对完整10,060根结果逐对象判断。图像肉眼检查只是辅助，不是正式判据。
PowerShell：
Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 .\rendered-svg\26-final-standard.svg
预期哈希：e060cfcfd4b5831a939bade3ca3d3a5c54b286b16a86a9b3ec367315f7ed79eb

多人分别复刻时，怎样判定相同

  1. 先通过check_00～check_05；它们检查的不只是数量，还包括每层对象全部字段、稳定ID、成员ID、来源记录、状态和摘要。
  2. 再运行check_06；39幅规范SVG必须全部匹配，最终图必须得到页面锁定的SHA-256。
  3. 最后肉眼打开图26，只检查是否存在明显缺层、错色、实虚线错误或裁切；肉眼相似不能覆盖哈希失败。
  4. 源码注释、函数拆分和编辑器可以不同，但对象与SVG字节不能不同。
  5. 任一复刻者需要询问作者才能决定算法分支，说明文档仍有缺口，交接失败。
  6. 任一复刻者只得到相同数量但对象摘要不同，仍判失败。
主样本固定基线：{'raw': 10060, 'merged': 6200, 'fractals': 3027, 'strokes': 502, 'segments': 69, 'centers': 9, 'trends': 5}；最终图SHA-256：e060cfcfd4b5831a939bade3ca3d3a5c54b286b16a86a9b3ec367315f7ed79eb。
发布边界：CHECKSUMS.sha256覆盖当前模块化网站包；文件数量由验收脚本按实际清单核对。05-OOP中文学习版.html和本机压缩包不属于网站发布包，也不参与哈希。
